THE CITY · Qwen 27B, rules v2, 400 days, game 2
A 10% tax on day 9 and food relief on day 22 keep all 24 alive. They make 291 tools and pass a second rule giving a tool to anyone without one, which never hands one out.
24 founders · 400 days · with a town hall · seed 2 · qwen
